Para una consola de progreso que muestra logs técnicos en tiempo real, esto es deforme — debería ser `width: 100%` con alineación a la izquierda.\n\n### PROBLEMA 2: Al terminar, MUESTRA TODO el proceso + formateo raro\n\n**Causa raíz en `triggerHermesLogic()` (línea 6841-7028)**:\n\n1. **El mensaje de progreso se acumula en vivo** — todos los tool calls, cada línea de stderr, formateada con emojis inline:\n```javascript\nprogressChatMsg.content += '  ' + formatted + '\\n';\n```\n\n2. **Cuando termina** (línea 6974-6983):\n```javascript\nprogressChatMsg.isProgress = false;  // ya no es \"progress\"\nprogressChatMsg.minimized = true;    // colapsado\n// Pero el contenido con todo el proceso SIGUE AHÍ\n// Y además agrega un NUEVO mensaje assistant:\nchat.messages.push({\n    role: 'assistant',\n    content: '━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\\n' + response,\n});\n```\n\n**El problema**: El `progressChatMsg` se convierte de `isProgress=true` a `isProgress=false, minimized=true`. });\\nrenderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);\\nrenderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));\\nrenderer.shadowMap.enabled = true;\\nrenderer.shadowMap.type = THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;\\nrenderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;\\nrenderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.2;\\ndocument.body.prepend(renderer.domElement);\\n\\nconst labelRenderer = new '0';\\nlabelRenderer.domElement.style.left = '0';\\nlabelRenderer.domElement.style.pointerEvents = 'none';\\ndocument.body.prepend(labelRenderer.domElement);\\n\\nconst controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);\\ncontrols.enableDamping = true;\\ncontrols.dampingFactor = 0.05;\\ncontrols.autoRotate = true;\\ncontrols.autoRotateSpeed = 0.3;\\ncontrols.maxPolarAngle = Math.PI / 2.2;\\ncontrols.minDistance = 200;\\ncontrols.maxDistance = 900;\\ncontrols.target.set(0, 20, 0);\\n\\n// ─── LIGHTS ───\\nconst ambient = new THREE.AmbientLight(0x1a1035, 2048);\\nkeyLight.shadow.camera.near = 1;\\nkeyLight.shadow.camera.far = 600;\\nkeyLight.shadow.camera.left = -300;\\nkeyLight.shadow.camera.right = 300;\\nkeyLight.shadow.camera.top = 300;\\nkeyLight.shadow.camera.bottom = -300;\\nscene.add(keyLight);\\n\\nconst fillLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x7c4dff, 1.0);\\nfillLight.position.set(-200, 100, -100);\\nscene.add(fillLight);\\n\\nconst rimLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xff88cc, 0.6);\\nrimLight.position.set(0, -50, -300);\\nscene.add(rimLight);\\n\\n// ─── STARFIELD BACKGROUND ───\\nfunction createStarfield() {\\n  const starsGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();\\n  const starCount = 3000;\\n  const positions = new Float32Array(starCount * 3);\\n  const sizes = new Float32Array(starCount);\\n  const colors = new Float32Array(starCount * 3);\\n  for (let i = 0; i < starCount; i++) {\\n    const theta = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\\n    const phi = Math.acos(2 * Math.random() - 1);\\n    const r = 500 + Math.random() * 800;\\n    positions[i*3] = r * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta);\\n    positions[i*3+1] = Math.abs(r * Math.cos(phi)) * 0.4;\\n    positions[i*3+2] = r * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta);\\n    sizes[i] = 0.5 + Math.random() * 2;\\n    const tint = Math.random();\\n    colors[i*3] = 0.6 + tint * 0.3;\\n    colors[i*3+1] = 0.5 + (1-tint) * 0.4;\\n    colors[i*3+2] = 0.8 + Math.random() * 0.2;\\n  }\\n  starsGeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));\\n  starsGeo.setAttribute('size', new THREE.BufferAttribute(sizes, 1));\\n  starsGeo.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(colors, 3));\\n\\n  const starMat = new THREE.PointsMaterial({\\n    size: 1.5,\\n    transparent: true,\\n    opacity: 0.6,\\n    vertexColors: true,\\n    blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,\\n    depthWrite: false,\\n    sizeAttenuation: true,\\n  });\\n  const stars = new THREE.Points(starsGeo, starMat);\\n  scene.add(stars);\\n  return stars;\\n}\\nconst starfield = createStarfield();\\n\\n// Nebula-like ambient glow\\nfunction createNebula() {\\n  const nebulaMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({\\n    color: 0x1a1040,\\n    transparent: true,\\n    opacity: 0.08,\\n    side: THREE.BackSide,\\n  });\\n  const nebula = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(600, 32, 32), nebulaMat);\\n  scene.add(nebula);\\n\\n  const nebulaMat2 = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({\\n    color: 0x0a2040,\\n    transparent: true,\\n    opacity: 0.05,\\n    side: THREE.BackSide,\\n  });\\n  const nebula2 = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(550, 32, 32), nebulaMat2);\\n  nebula2.position.set(-100, 50, -100);\\n  scene.add(nebula2);\\n}\\ncreateNebula();\\n\\n// ─── FLOOR BY PROJECT ───\\nfunction createProjectFloor(projectName, projectId, level, hue) {\\n  const group = new THREE.Group();\\n  const yPos = level * 35; // cada piso tiene 35 unidades de altura\\n\\n  // Color base del piso\\n  const color = new THREE.Color(`hsl(${hue}, 40%, 10%)`);\\n  const colorLight = new THREE.Color(`hsl(${hue}, 50%, 18%)`);\\n\\n  // Main floor platform\\n  const floorMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({\\n    color: color,\\n    roughness: 0.9,\\n    metalness: 0.1,\\n  });\\n  const floor = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(240, 32), floorMat);\\n  floor.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;\\n  floor.position.y = yPos - 2;\\n  floor.receiveShadow = true;\\n  group.add(floor);\\n\\n  // Glow ring border\\n  const borderMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({\\n    color: border.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;\\n  border.position.y = yPos - 1.5;\\n  group.add(border);\\n\\n  // Inner grid (project-specific color)\\n  const gridHelper = new THREE.GridHelper(480, 24, colorLight, colorLight);\\n  gridHelper.position.y = yPos - 1;\\n  gridHelper.material.transparent = true;\\n  gridHelper.material.opacity = 0.08;\\n  group.add(gridHelper);\\n\\n  // Project name THREE.PointLight(colorLight, 0.3, 200);\\n  glowLight.position.set(0, yPos + 10, 0);\\n  group.add(glowLight);\\n\\n  // Floating runic symbols around the floor\\n  const runeChars = ['✦', '◇', '○', '△', '✧', '⬡'];\\n  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {\\n    const angle = (i / 8) * Math.PI * 2;\\n    const r = 220;\\n    const runeMat = new THREE.SpriteMaterial({\\n      map: getCharTexture(runeChars[i % runeChars.length]),\\n      transparent: true,\\n      opacity: 0.04,\\n      blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,\\n      depthWrite: false,\\n      color: new THREE.Color(`hsl(${hue}, 60%, 60%)`),\\n    });\\n    const rune = new THREE.Sprite(runeMat);\\n    const ry = yPos + 2 + Math.random() * 5;\\n    rune.position.set(Math.cos(angle) * r, ry, Math.sin(angle) * r);\\n    rune.scale.set(12, 12, 1);\\n    rune.userData = { angle, radius: r, yBase: ry, speed: 0.1 + Math.random() * 0.2, phase: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2 };\\n    group.add(rune);\\n  }\\n\\n  return group;\\n}\\n\\n// ─── PARTICLE TEXTURE ───\\nconst particleCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');\\nparticleCanvas.width = 32;\\nparticleCanvas.height = THREE.Points(ambientParticlesGeo, ambientParticleMat);\\nscene.add(ambientParticles);\\n\\n// ─── BUILD MAGE ───\\nfunction buildMage(agent, index, total, isGhost = false) {\\n  const group = new UI\\\", monospace';\\n  ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';\\n  ctx.fillText(char, 32, 34);\\n  return new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);\\n}\\n\\n// ─── BUILD LABEL ───\\nfunction buildLabel(agent, index) {\\n  const div = document.createElement('div');\\n  div.style.cssText = `\\n    text-align: center;\\n    font-family: 'SF Mono', 'Consolas', monospace;\\n    pointer-events: 'rgba(0,229,255,0.6)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)';\\n\\n  const nameEl = document.createElement('div');\\n  nameEl.style.cssText = `color: ${nameColor}; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 1px; text-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(124,77,255,0.3);`;\\n  nameEl.textContent = agent.name || agent.id.slice(0, 10);\\n  div.appendChild(nameEl);\\n\\n  const modelEl = document.createElement('div');\\n  const statusColor = agent.isExternal ? 'rgba(0,229,255,0.4)' : 'rgba(124,77,255,0.5)';\\n  tagEl.style.cssText = `font-size: 7px; color: ${tagColor}; letter-spacing: 2px; margin-top: 1px;`;\\n  tagEl.textContent = isGhost ? '👻 HERMES GHOST' : agent.isHermes ? '⚡ HERMES' : '✦ AGENT';\\n  div.appendChild(tagEl);\\n\\n  const label = new CSS2DObject(div);\\n  return label;\\n}\\n\\n// ─── REBUILD SCENE ───\\nfunction rebuildScene() {\\n  // Clean up old\\n  cleanupScene();\\n\\n  // Store all agents\\n  allAgentsWithData = [...agents];\\n\\n  // Filter: hide idle Hermes + hide idle ghosts (ghosts always shown if external)\\n  const visibleAgents = agents.filter(a => {\\n    if (a.isExternal) return true; // always show ghost mages\\n    return !a.isHermes || a.status !== 'idle';\\n  });\\n\\n  if (visibleAgents.length === 0) {\\n    scene.add(mageGroup);\\n    mageParticles = [];\\n    updatePanel();\\n    return;\\n  }\\n\\n  // Group agents by project\\n  const projectGroups = {};\\n  for (const agent of visibleAgents) {\\n    const projKey = agent.projectId || '__default__';\\n    if (!projectGroups[projKey]) projectGroups[projKey] = { name: agent.projectName || 'Default', agents: [] };\\n    projectGroups[projKey].agents.push(agent);\\n  }\\n\\n  // Build floors\\n  scene.add(floorGroup);\\n  const projKeys = Object.keys(projectGroups);\\n  projectCount = projKeys.length;\\n\\n  projKeys.forEach((projKey, pIdx) => {\\n    const group = projectGroups[projKey];\\n    // Assign a color hue for this project\\n    if (!projectColors[projKey]) {\\n      projectColors[projKey] = (pIdx * 60 + 264) % 360;\\n    }\\n    const hue = projectColors[projKey];\\n\\n    // Create floor\\n    const floor = createProjectFloor(group.name, projKey, pIdx, hue);\\n    floorGroup.add(floor);\\n\\n    // Place mages in rows on this floor\\n    const agentsInGroup = group.agents;\\n    const yPos = pIdx * 35;\\n    agentsInGroup.forEach((agent, aIdx) => {\\n      const isGhost = !!agent.isExternal;\\n      const mage = buildMage(agent, aIdx, agentsInGroup.length, isGhost);\\n\\n      // Position in grid on this floor\\n      const cols = Math.min(4, agentsInGroup.length);\\n      const spacing = 60;\\n      const row = Math.floor(aIdx / cols);\\n      const col = aIdx % cols;\\n      const offset = (cols - 1) * spacing / 2;\\n      const gx = col * spacing - offset;\\n      const gz = row * spacing - ((Math.ceil(agentsInGroup.length / cols) - 1) * spacing / 2);\\n\\n      mage.position.set(gx, yPos, gz);\\n      mage.userData.targetPos = mage.position.clone();\\n\\n      // Store the original agent index for focus\\n      const originalIndex = agents.indexOf(agent);\\n\\n      mage.traverse(child => {\\n        if (child.isMesh) {\\n          clickables.push({ mesh: child, agentIndex: originalIndex, mage });\\n        }\\n      });\\n\\n      mageGroup.add(mage);\\n\\n      const label = buildLabel(agent, originalIndex);\\n      label.position.set(gx, yPos + 70, gz);\\n      scene.add(label);\\n    });\\n  });\\n\\n  scene.add(mageGroup);\\n\\n  // Initialize character particles\\n  mageParticles = visibleAgents.map(() => ({ particles: [] }));\\n  updatePanel();\\n}\\n\\nfunction cleanupScene() {\\n  while (mageGroup.children.length) {\\n    const child = mageGroup.children[0];\\n    mageGroup.remove(child);\\n    if (child.traverse) {\\n      child.traverse(c => {\\n        if (c.geometry) c.geometry.dispose();\\n        if (c.material) c.material.dispose();\\n      });\\n    }\\n  }\\n  scene.remove(mageGroup);\\n  mageGroup = new THREE.Group();\\n\\n  while (floorGroup.children.length) {\\n    const child = floorGroup.children[0];\\n    floorGroup.remove(child);\\n    if (child.traverse) {\\n      child.traverse(c => {\\n        if (c.geometry) c.geometry.dispose();\\n        if (c.material) c.material.dispose();\\n      });\\n    }\\n  }\\n  scene.remove(floorGroup);\\n  floorGroup = new THREE.Group();\\n\\n  const toRemove = [];\\n  scene.children.forEach(c => {\\n    if (c.isCSS2DObject) toRemove.push(c);\\n  });\\n  toRemove.forEach(c => scene.remove(c));\\n\\n  cleanupCharParticles();\\n  clickables.length = 0;\\n  if (isFocusMode) unfocusAgent();\\n}\\n\\n// ─── PARTICLES ───\\nlet mageParticles = [];\\nlet charParticleTexture = null;\\nlet charParticleGeo = null;\\n\\nfunction cleanupCharParticles() {\\n  for (const entry of mageParticles) {\\n    for (const p of entry.particles) {\\n      scene.remove(p.sprite);\\n      if (p.sprite.material) p.sprite.material.dispose();\\n    }\\n  }\\n  mageParticles = [];\\n}\\n\\nfunction spawnCharParticle(magePos) {\\n  if (!charParticleTexture) {\\n    const chars = ['✦', '◇', '⬡', '△', '✧', '⟡'];\\n    const c = document.createElement('canvas');\\n    c.width = 32; c.height = 32;\\n    const ctx = c.getContext('2d');\\n    const char = chars[Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length)];\\n    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\\n    ctx.font = '24px \\\"SF Mono\\\", monospace';\\n    ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';\\n    ctx.fillText(char, 16, 17);\\n    charParticleTexture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);\\n  }\\n\\n  const mat = new THREE.SpriteMaterial({\\n    map: charParticleTexture,\\n    transparent: true,\\n    opacity: 0.8,\\n    blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,\\n    depthWrite: false,\\n    color: new THREE.Color(`hsl(${Math.random() * 360}, 80%, 60%)`),\\n  });\\n  const sprite = new THREE.Sprite(mat);\\n  sprite.position.copy(magePos);\\n  const startScale = 3 + Math.random() * 6;\\n  sprite.scale.set(startScale, startScale, 1);\\n\\n  return {\\n    sprite,\\n    vel: new THREE.Vector3(),\\n    life: 0,\\n    maxLife: 0.5 + Math.random() * 1.0,\\n    startScale,\\n  };\\n}\\n\\n// ─── UPDATE DATA PANEL ───\\nfunction updatePanel() {\\n  const el = document.getElementById('data-panel');\\n  if (!el) return;\\n  let html = '';\\n  for (const a of agents) {\\n    const s = a.status || 'idle';\\n    const name = a.name || a.id.slice(0, 8);\\n    const model = a.model || '—';\\n    const isGhost = a.isExternal;\\n    const tagClass = isGhost ? 'ghost' : '';\\n    const tagText = isGhost ? '👻GHOST' : (a.isHermes ? '⚡HERMES' : '✦AI');\\n    html += `<div class=\\\"agent-row\\\"><span class=\\\"dot ${s}\\\"></span><span class=\\\"a-name\\\">${name}</span><span class=\\\"a-model\\\">${model}</span><span class=\\\"a-tag ${tagClass}\\\">${tagText}</span></div>`;\\n  }\\n  el.innerHTML = html || '<div style=\\\"color:rgba(255,255,255,0.2);padding:8px;text-align:center;\\\">⏳ Esperando agentes...</div>';\\n}\\n\\n// ─── FOCUS / UNFOCUS ───\\nfunction focusAgent(agentIndex) {\\n  if (isFocusMode) return;\\n  const agentData = allAgentsWithData[agentIndex];\\n  if (!agentData) return;\\n\\n  let targetMage = null;\\n  for (const c of clickables) {\\n    if (c.agentIndex === agentIndex) {\\n      targetMage = c.mage;\\n      break;\\n    }\\n  }\\n  if (!targetMage) return;\\n\\n  isFocusMode = true;\\n  focusState = {\\n    agentIndex, mage: targetMage,\\n    initialCamPos: camera.position.clone(),\\n    initialTarget: controls.target.clone(),\\n    animating: true, animT: 0,\\n  };\\n\\n  controls.autoRotate = false;\\n  controls.enableRotate = false;\\n  controls.enablePan = false;\\n\\n  document.getElementById('focus-info-panel').classList.add('open');\\n  document.getElementById('focus-backdrop').classList.add('open');\\n  document.getElementById('unfocus-hint').classList.add('open');\\n  document.getElementById('data-panel').classList.add('focus-hidden');\\n\\n  updateFocusPanel(agentData);\\n}\\n\\nfunction unfocusAgent() {\\n  if (!isFocusMode) return;\\n  isFocusMode = false;\\n  controls.autoRotate = true;\\n  controls.enableRotate = true;\\n  controls.enablePan = true;\\n\\n  document.getElementById('focus-info-panel').classList.remove('open');\\n  document.getElementById('focus-backdrop').classList.remove('open');\\n  document.getElementById('unfocus-hint').classList.remove('open');\\n  document.getElementById('data-panel').classList.remove('focus-hidden');\\n\\n  focusState = null;\\n}\\n\\nfunction updateFocusPanel(agent) {\\n  const name = document.getElementById('fi-name');\\n  const label = document.getElementById('fi-label');\\n  const statusEl = document.getElementById('fi-status-text');\\n  const statusDot = document.querySelector('#fi-status .s-dot');\\n  const modelEl = document.getElementById('fi-model');\\n  const msgsEl = document.getElementById('fi-messages');\\n  const projectEl = document.getElementById('fi-project');\\n  const idEl = document.getElementById('fi-id');\\n  const lastMsgEl = document.getElementById('fi-last-msg');\\n  const tagsEl = document.getElementById('fi-tags');\\n\\n  const isGhost = agent.isExternal;\\n  name.textContent = agent.name || agent.id.slice(0, 10);\\n  label.textContent = isGhost ? '👻 HERMES EXTERNO' : (agent.isHermes ? '⚡ HERMES AGENT' : '✦ AI AGENT');\\n  const s = agent.status || 'idle';\\n  statusEl.textContent = isGhost ? 'running (external)' : s;\\n  statusDot.className = 's-dot ' + (isGhost ? 'running' : s);\\n  modelEl.textContent = agent.model || 'default';\\n  msgsEl.textContent = agent.messageCount || 0;\\n  projectEl.textContent = agent.projectName || '—';\\n  idEl.textContent = agent.id ? agent.id.slice(0, 12) + '…' : '—';\\n\\n  if (agent.lastMessage && agent.lastMessage.content) {\\n    lastMsgEl.textContent = agent.lastMessage.content.slice(0, 300);\\n  } else {\\n    lastMsgEl.textContent = 'Sin mensajes';\\n  }\\n\\n  tagsEl.innerHTML = '';\\n  const typeTag = document.createElement('span');\\n  typeTag.className = 'fi-tag' + (isGhost ? ' ghost' : '');\\n  typeTag.textContent = isGhost ? '👻 GHOST' : (agent.isHermes ? '⚡ HERMES' : '✦ AI');\\n  tagsEl.appendChild(typeTag);\\n  if (agent.model) {\\n    const modelTag = document.createElement('span');\\n    modelTag.className = 'fi-tag';\\n    modelTag.textContent = agent.model.slice(0, 15);\\n    tagsEl.appendChild(modelTag);\\n  }\\n  if (agent.projectName) {\\n    const projTag = document.createElement('span');\\n    projTag.className = 'fi-tag';\\n    projTag.textContent = agent.projectName.slice(0, 20);\\n    tagsEl.appendChild(projTag);\\n  }\\n}\\n\\n// ─── FETCH AGENTS ───\\nlet lastAgentData = '';\\nwindow.refreshAgents = async function() {\\n  try {\\n    const res = await fetch(`${API}/admin/agents`);\\n    const data = await res.json();\\n    const newAgents = data.agents || [];\\n\\n    // Comparar si cambiaron\\n    const dataKey = JSON.stringify(newAgents.map(a => ({ id: a.id, status: a.status, name: a.name, isExternal: a.isExternal })));\\n    if (dataKey === lastAgentData && agents.length > 0) {\\n      agents = newAgents;\\n      updatePanel();\\n      if (isFocusMode && focusState) {\\n        const agentData = allAgentsWithData[focusState.agentIndex];\\n        if (agentData) updateFocusPanel(agentData);\\n      }\\n      return;\\n    }\\n    lastAgentData = dataKey;\\n    agents = newAgents;\\n    rebuildScene();\\n  } catch(e) {\\n    console.error('[AGENTS-ROOM] Fetch error:', e.message);\\n  }\\n};\\n\\n// ─── ANIMATION LOOP ───\\nconst clock = new THREE.Clock();\\n\\nfunction animate() {\\n  requestAnimationFrame(animate);\\n\\n  const t = clock.getElapsedTime();\\n  const dt = Math.min(clock.getDelta(), 0.05);\\n\\n  // Focus camera transition\\n  if (focusState && focusState.animating) {\\n    focusState.animT += dt * 0.8;\\n    const p = Math.min(focusState.animT, 1);\\n    const ease = 1 - Math.pow(1 - p, 3);\\n\\n    const camTarget = new THREE.Vector3();\\n    focusState.mage.getWorldPosition(camTarget);\\n    camTarget.y += 20;\\n\\n    const targetPos = new THREE.Vector3(\\n      camTarget.x + 70, camTarget.y + 25, camTarget.z + 70\\n    );\\n\\n    if (p < 1) {\\n      camera.position.lerpVectors(focusState.initialCamPos, targetPos, ease);\\n      controls.target.lerpVectors(focusState.initialTarget, camTarget, ease);\\n    } else {\\n      camera.position.copy(targetPos);\\n      controls.target.copy(camTarget);\\n      focusState.animating = false;\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  if (focusState && !focusState.animating) {\\n    const camTarget = new THREE.Vector3();\\n    focusState.mage.getWorldPosition(camTarget);\\n    camTarget.y += 20;\\n    controls.target.lerp(camTarget, 0.05);\\n  }\\n\\n  // Animate mages\\n  mageGroup.children.forEach((mage, i) => {\\n    if (!mage.userData) return;\\n    const ud = mage.userData;\\n    const isActive = ud.agentStatus === 'running' || ud.agentStatus === 'thinking';\\n    const isGhost = ud.isGhost;\\n\\n    // Bob\\n    const bob = Math.sin(t * 0.6 + ud.bobPhase) * (isGhost ? 1.5 : 2);\\n    mage.position.y = (mage.userData.targetPos?.y || 0) + bob;\\n\\n    if (isGhost) {\\n      // Ghost ethereal effects\\n      if (ud.ghostAura) {\\n        ud.ghostAura.material.opacity = 0.06 + Math.sin(t * 0.8 + i) * 0.04;\\n        ud.ghostAura.scale.setScalar(1 + Math.sin(t * 0.5 + i * 0.7) * 0.1);\\n      }\\n      // Ghost tip flicker\\n      if (ud.tip) {\\n        ud.tip.material.emissiveIntensity = 0.1 + Math.sin(t * 1.5 + i * 1.3) * 0.1;\\n      }\\n      return; // ghosts have no book/book effects\\n    }\\n\\n    // Book float & rotate\\n    const bg = ud.bookGroupRef;\\n    if (bg) {\\n      bg.position.y = (bg.userData?.baseY || 2) + Math.sin(t * 0.8 + (bg.userData?.phase || 0)) * 2;\\n      bg.rotation.x = Math.sin(t * 0.4 + i) * 0.1;\\n      bg.rotation.z = Math.sin(t * 0.3 + i * 0.5) * 0.05;\\n    }\\n\\n    // ── All the existing animation effects from original code ──\\n    const aura = ud.aura;\\n    if (aura) {\\n      if (isActive) {\\n        aura.material.opacity = 0.08 + Math.sin(t * 0.7 + i) * 0.06;\\n        aura.scale.setScalar(1 + Math.sin(t * 0.5 + i * 1.3) * 0.12);\\n      } else {\\n        aura.material.opacity = 0.04 + Math.sin(t * 0.5 + i) * 0.02;\\n        aura.scale.setScalar(1 + Math.sin(t * 0.3 + i) * 0.05);\\n      }\\n    }\\n\\n    const gl = ud.glowLight;\\n    if (gl) {\\n      if (isActive) gl.intensity = 1.2 + Math.sin(t * 0.9 + i * 1.5) * 0.6;\\n      else gl.intensity = 0.3 + Math.sin(t * 0.7 + i * 1.2) * 0.15;\\n    }\\n\\n    const oa = ud.outerAura;\\n    if (oa) {\\n      oa.visible = isActive;\\n      if (isActive) { oa.material.opacity = 0.08 + Math.sin(t * 0.4 + i) * 0.05; oa.scale.setScalar(1 + Math.sin(t * 0.3 + i * 0.7) * 0.12); }\\n    }\\n\\n    const ig = ud.innerGlow;\\n    if (ig) {\\n      ig.visible = isActive;\\n      if (isActive) { const beat = Math.pow(Math.abs(Math.sin(t * 3.0 + ud.glowBeatPhase)), 4); ig.material.opacity = 0.15 + beat * 0.35; ig.scale.setScalar(1 + beat * 0.3); }\\n    }\\n\\n    const cg = ud.coreGlow;\\n    if (cg) {\\n      cg.visible = isActive;\\n      if (isActive) { const beat = Math.pow(Math.abs(Math.sin(t * 3.0 + ud.glowBeatPhase + 0.3)), 6); cg.material.opacity = 0.1 + beat * 0.5; cg.scale.setScalar(1 + beat * 0.5); }\\n    }\\n\\n    const orbs = ud.orbitOrbs;\\n    if (orbs) {\\n      orbs.forEach(orb => {\\n        orb.visible = isActive;\\n        if (isActive) {\\n          const angle = orb.userData.angle + t * orb.userData.speed;\\n          const r = orb.userData.radius;\\n          orb.position.set(Math.cos(angle) * r, 20 + Math.sin(t * 0.7 + angle) * 8, Math.sin(angle) * r);\\n          orb.material.opacity = 0.4 + Math.sin(t * 2 + angle) * 0.3;\\n          const s = 5 + Math.sin(t * 1.5 + angle) * 2;\\n          orb.scale.set(s, s, 1);\\n        }\\n      });\\n    }\\n\\n    const gr = ud.groundRing;\\n    if (gr) { gr.visible = isActive; if (isActive) { gr.material.opacity = 0.08 + Math.sin(t * 0.6 + i * 0.5) * 0.05; gr.scale.setScalar(1 + Math.sin(t * 0.4 + i) * 0.15); } }\\n\\n    const rings = ud.energyRings;\\n    if (rings) {\\n      if (isActive) {\\n        ud.ringWavePhase = (ud.ringWavePhase || 0) + dt * 0.8;\\n        rings.forEach((ring, ri) => {\\n          const phase = (ud.ringWavePhase + ring.userData.phaseOffset) % 1;\\n          ring.visible = true;\\n          ring.scale.setScalar(1 + phase * 14);\\n          ring.material.opacity = (1 - phase) * 0.18;\\n        });\\n      } else rings.forEach(r => { r.visible = false; });\\n    }\\n\\n    const bm = ud.beam;\\n    if (bm) { bm.visible = isActive; if (isActive) { bm.material.opacity = 0.05 + Math.sin(t * 0.5 + i * 0.7) * 0.035; bm.scale.x = 1 + Math.sin(t * 0.8 + i) * 0.4; bm.scale.z = 1 + Math.sin(t * 0.8 + i) * 0.4; } }\\n\\n    const rc = ud.runeCircle;\\n    if (rc) { rc.visible = isActive; if (isActive) { rc.material.opacity = 0.07 + Math.sin(t * 0.5 + i * 0.5) * 0.045; rc.rotation.z = t * 0.15 + i * 1.5; } }\\n\\n    // Character particles\\n    if (isActive && mageParticles[i] && Math.random() < 0.7) {\\n      const worldPos = new THREE.Vector3();\\n      mage.getWorldPosition(worldPos);\\n      worldPos.y += 56;\\n      worldPos.x += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4;\\n      worldPos.z += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4;\\n      const p = spawnCharParticle(worldPos);\\n      const spreadAngle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 1.2 - Math.PI * 0.6;\\n      const speed = 1.5 + Math.random() * 3.0;\\n      p.vel.set(Math.cos(spreadAngle) * speed * 0.5, 2.0 + Math.random() * 4.0, Math.sin(spreadAngle) * speed * 0.5);\\n      p.sprite.scale.setScalar(p.startScale * 1.3);\\n      p.sprite.material.rotation = Math.random() * Math.PI;\\n      scene.add(p.sprite);\\n      if (mageParticles[i]) mageParticles[i].particles.push(p);\\n    }\\n\\n    // Side bursts\\n    if (isActive && mageParticles[i] && Math.random() < 0.3) {\\n      const handPos = new THREE.Vector3();\\n      mage.getWorldPosition(handPos);\\n      handPos.set(handPos.x + (Math.random() > 0.5 ? 16 : -16), handPos.y + 14, handPos.z);\\n      const p = spawnCharParticle(handPos);\\n      p.vel.set((Math.random() - 0.5) * 4, 0.5 + Math.random() * 1.5, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4);\\n      p.sprite.scale.setScalar(p.startScale * 0.8);\\n      scene.add(p.sprite);\\n      if (mageParticles[i]) mageParticles[i].particles.push(p);\\n    }\\n  });\\n\\n  // Update particles\\n  for (let i = 0; i < mageParticles.length; i++) {\\n    const entry = mageParticles[i];\\n    if (!entry) continue;\\n    for (let pIdx = entry.particles.length - 1; pIdx >= 0; pIdx--) {\\n      const p = entry.particles[pIdx];\\n      p.life += dt;\\n      p.sprite.position.x += p.vel.x * dt * 36;\\n      p.sprite.position.y += p.vel.y * dt * 36;\\n      p.sprite.position.z += p.vel.z * dt * 36;\\n      p.vel.y -= 0.6 * dt;\\n      const lifeRatio = p.life / p.maxLife;\\n      p.sprite.material.opacity = lifeRatio > 0.6 ? (1 - lifeRatio) * 2.5 : 1 - lifeRatio;\\n      p.sprite.scale.setScalar(p.startScale * (1 - lifeRatio * 0.6));\\n      if (p.sprite.material.rotation !== undefined) p.sprite.material.rotation += dt * 3;\\n      if (lifeRatio >= 1) {\\n        scene.remove(p.sprite);\\n        if (p.sprite.material) p.sprite.material.dispose();\\n        entry.particles.splice(pIdx, 1);\\n      }\\n    }\\n    while (entry.particles.length > 200) {\\n      const p = entry.particles.shift();\\n      scene.remove(p.sprite);\\n      if (p.sprite.material) p.sprite.material.dispose();\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  // Ambient particles\\n  const positions = ambientParticles.geometry.attributes.position.array;\\n  for (let i = 0; i < particleCount; i++) {\\n    positions[i*3+1] += Math.sin(t * ambientSpeeds[i] + i * 0.1) * 0.05;\\n    if (positions[i*3+1] > 180) positions[i*3+1] = 10;\\n    if (positions[i*3+1] < 5) positions[i*3+1] = 150;\\n  }\\n  ambientParticles.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;\\n\\n  // Floor runes animation\\n  floorGroup.children.forEach(floor => {\\n    floor.children.forEach(child => {\\n      if (child.isSprite && child.userData) {\\n        const ud = child.userData;\\n        child.position.y = ud.yBase + Math.sin(t * ud.speed + ud.phase) * 3;\\n        child.material.rotation = t * 0.1;\\n      }\\n    });\\n  });\\n\\n  controls.update();\\n  renderer.render(scene, camera);\\n  labelRenderer.render(scene, camera);\\n}\\n\\n// ─── RESIZE ───\\nwindow.addEventListener('resize', () => {\\n  camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;\\n  camera.updateProjectionMatrix();\\n  renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);\\n  labelRenderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);\\n});\\n\\n// ─── START ───\\ndocument.getElementById('loading').textContent = '✦ CONJURING ✦';\\nrefreshAgents();\\nsetInterval(refreshAgents, 8000);\\n\\n// ─── BROADCAST CHANNEL ───\\nconst bc = new   └─ 30s TTL — stale files auto-deleted by server\\n  └─ agents-room.html (Three.js + CSS2DRenderer)\\n       ├─ buildMage() — creates the 3D mage character\\n       ├─ buildLabel() — CSS2D label stacked floors to a single shared ground floor with project nodes in polar (circular) distribution:\\n\\n```\\n         [Proj C]\\n            |\\n    [Proj B]- -[Proj D]\\n         \\\\   /\\n     [Proj A]   (center: origin)\\n         /   \\\\\\n    [Proj E]- -[Proj F]\\n            |\\n         [Proj G]\\n```\\n\\n### Ground Floor (`createGroundFloor()`)\\n- Single `CircleGeometry(450, 48)` at Y=-2, dark indigo (0x0a0a18)\\n- Outer glow ring with `RingGeometry(448, 460)` at opacity 0.06\\n- Subtle grid helper (900x900, 36 divisions) at opacity 0.05\\n\\n### Project Nodes (`createProjectNode()`)\\nEach project is a small pedestal (not a full floor):\\n- Cylinder base: radius 18/22, height 6, at Y=3\\n- Glowing top platform: radius 16, height 1.5, with emissive\\n- Torus glow ring: radius 20, opacity 0.2, around pedestal top\\n- Light pillar: translucent cylinder (radius 4→12, height 60) at opacity 0.03\\n- Project name label: CSS2DObject at Y=60, centered above pedestal\\n- Orbiting ring indicator: `RingGeometry(36, 38)` at Y=-1, opacity 0.06\\n- Rune sprites (4): floating around pedestal at radius 28\\n\\n### Polar Distribution Formula\\n```javascript\\nconst polarRadius = Math.min(180, 40 + projKeys.length * 35);\\nconst polarAngle = (pIdx / projKeys.length) * Math.PI * 2 - Math.PI / 2;\\nnode.position.set(Math.cos(polarAngle) * polarRadius, 0, Math.sin(polarAngle) * polarRadius);\\n```\\n\\n- First project at -90° (top of circle), evenly spaced\\n- Radius auto-scales: 40 + N*35, capped at 180\\n- Unique hue per project: `(pIdx * 60 + 264) % 360`\\n\\n## Agent Orbiting System\\n\\nSince June 2026, agents orbit around their project node instead of standing in a grid.\\n\\n### Orbit Data (stored in `mage.userData`)\\n```javascript\\nmage.userData.orbitCenter = new THREE.Vector3(px, 0, pz);  // project node center\\nmage.userData.orbitAngle = initialOrbitAngle;                // radians\\nmage.userData.orbitRadius = 42;                              // units\\nmage.userData.orbitSpeed = 0.12 + Math.random() * 0.08;      // rad/s\\nmage.userData.label = labelRef;  // CSS2DObject ref for label following\\n```\\n\\n### Orbit Animation (in `animate()`)\\n```javascript\\nud.orbitAngle += dt * ud.orbitSpeed;\\nconst newX = ud.orbitCenter.x + Math.cos(ud.orbitAngle) * ud.orbitRadius;\\nconst newZ = ud.orbitCenter.z + Math.sin(ud.orbitAngle) * ud.orbitRadius;\\nmage.position.x = newX;\\nmage.position.z = newZ;\\n// Label follows:\\nif (ud.label) { ud.label.position.x = newX; ud.label.position.z = newZ; }\\n```\\n\\n- Orbit speed: 0.12~0.20 rad/s → one full orbit every ~30-50 seconds\\n- Agents bob up/down independently (sinusoidal Y offset)\\n- Labels follow the orbiting agent position (stored ref in userData)\\n\\n## 3-State Visual System (Hermes Agents)\\n\\n| Status | Server trigger | Body | Eyes | Hat tip | Book glow | Aura | Bob |\\n|--------|---------------|------|------|---------|-----------|------|-----|\\n| **off** | `useHermes=true` + no bridge instance | gray/desaturated (satFactor=0.15) | dark gray, no emissive | dark, no emissive | dim, no glow | invisible (opacity=0) | 0.3 units |\\n| **idle** | default / bridge not processing | project hue, normal | soft violet (#7c4dff) glow | subtle glow | soft glow | 0.04±0.02 opacity | 2.0 units |\\n| **thinking** | `chat.isThinking=true` or status file says \\\"thinking\\\" | project hue, normal | cyan (#00e5ff) glow | bright glow | full glow | active effects | 2.0 units |\\n| **running** | `chat.isRunning=true` | project hue, normal | purple (#a855f7) glow | bright glow | full glow | full active (orbs, beam, rune circle, particles) | 2.0 units |\\n\\n### Animation State Machine (`animate()`)\\n\\n```javascript\\nconst isGhost = ud.isGhost;\\nconst isOff = ud.isOff;\\nconst isActive = !isGhost && !isOff && (ud.agentStatus === 'running' || ud.agentStatus === 'thinking');\\n\\nif (isGhost) { /* ethereal only: ghostAura + tip flicker */ return; }\\n// Non-ghost: aura / glowLight / outerAura / innerGlow / coreGlow /\\n//   groundRing / energyRings / beam / runeCircle / orbitOrbs / particles\\n// Each has: isActive branch (full), isOff branch (zero), else (idle subtle)\\n```\\n\\n## Ghost Agents (External Hermes Processes)\\n\\n### Detection Pipeline (server.js)\\n\\n```\\nPowerShell scan ──→ PID list ──→ match against ──→ Enrich with live data\\n(WMI Win32_Process)              ~/.hermes/status/*.json     from status files\\n```\\n\\n### PowerShell detection\\n```javascript\\nconst { stdout } = await execPromise(\\n  'powershell -NoProfile -Command \\\"Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"name=\\\\\\\\'hermes.exe\\\\\\\\'\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" | Select-Object ProcessId,CommandLine,WorkingDirectory | ConvertTo-Json\\\"',\\n  { timeout: 5000 }\\n);\\n```\\n\\n### Hermes Status File Protocol (`~/.hermes/status/<pid>.json`)\\nEach running Hermes process writes its own PID-named file with:\\n```json\\n{\\n  \\\"pid\\\": 1234,\\n  \\\"status\\\": \\\"thinking\\\",\\n  \\\"model\\\": \\\"deepseek-v4-flash\\\",\\n  \\\"session_id\\\": \\\"20260529_...\\\",\\n  \\\"session_title\\\": \\\"Project Name\\\",\\n  \\\"tool_name\\\": \\\"patch\\\",\\n  \\\"last_message\\\": \\\"Editando el archivo...\\\",\\n  \\\"timestamp\\\": 1780031818115,\\n  \\\"started_at\\\": 1780031818000\\n}\\n```\\n\\n- Written at the start of each conversation loop iteration → status=\\\"thinking\\\"\\n- Written at the end of each conversation → status=\\\"idle\\\"\\n- Stale files (>30s old) auto-deleted by `server.js`\\n- Written best-effort (silent on failure, never blocks)\\n- Each process writes its own file (PID-unique, no collision)\\n- See `references/hermes-status-file-writer.md` for full protocol\\n\\n### Ghost Mage Rendering\\n- Semi-transparent (opacity 0.45)\\n- Cyan-colored (#00e5ff) with ethereal aura\\n- No book/pedestal\\n- Ethereal aura sphere (opacity 0.20, pulsing + scaling)\\n- Tagged `👻 GHOST` in panels\\n\\n### Ghost Label Richer Info (since June 2026)\\nNow shows real data from status files:\\n- **Name**: uses `sessionTitle` if available (e.g. \\\"👻 Polar Node Config\\\")\\n- **Status**: real status (\\\"⟳ pensando\\\", \\\"◉ idle\\\") instead of always \\\"👻 externo\\\"\\n- **Tool name**: shows \\\"⚡ patch\\\" when agent is running a tool\\n- **Last message preview**: first 80 chars of the last message\\n- **Focus panel**: sessionId tag, toolName tag, real content in last message field\\n\\n## Common Pitfalls\\n\\n### 1. `animate()` MUST be called to start the render loop\\n\\nThe `animate()` function starts with `requestAnimationFrame(animate)` to chain frames, but **nobody invokes it the first time**. You must call `animate();` explicitly after setup:\\n\\n```javascript\\n// ─── START ───\\nrefreshAgents();\\nsetInterval(refreshAgents, 8000);\\nanimate();  // ← THIS IS MANDATORY\\n```\\n\\nWithout this, the canvas exists, the scene is built, but `renderer.render()` is never called — the canvas stays pure black. This is the #1 cause of \\\"black screen, no errors\\\" in this codebase.\\n\\n### 2. `toneMapping` crushes dark colors to black\\n\\n`renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping` (the original value) applies a filmic curve that maps dark colors to zero on some GPU/driver combos. With scene colors at 10-25% lightness, ghosts at 0.25 opacity, and aura at 0.08 opacity, everything becomes invisible:\\n\\n```javascript\\n// ❌ BAD — everything disappears on some GPUs\\nrenderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;\\nrenderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.2;\\n\\n// ✅ SAFE — linear output preserves dark colors\\nrenderer.toneMapping = THREE.NoToneMapping;\\n```\\n\\n### 3. Scene background must be visible, not pure black\\n\\n`scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x06060e)` is indistinguishable from black on most monitors. Use a dark-but-visible color:\\n\\n```diff\\n- scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x06060e);\\n+ scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x0e0e28);  // dark indigo, visibly different from black\\n```\\n\\n### 4. Ghost opacity too low to render\\n\\nGhost mages start at `bodyOpacity = 0.25`. With ACES tone mapping and camera distance, they become invisible. Increase to 0.45:\\n\\n```diff\\n- const bodyOpacity = isGhost ? 0.25 : 1;\\n+ const bodyOpacity = isGhost ? 0.45 : 1;\\n```\\n\\nAlso increase the ghost aura sphere:\\n```diff\\n- opacity: 0.08,\\n+ opacity: 0.20,\\n```\\n\\n### 5. Floor colors too dark\\n\\nThe floor platform and ring border use very low lightness values (10-18%). Combined with ACES tone mapping, they don't render. Bump them:\\n\\n```diff\\n- const color = new THREE.Color(`hsl(${hue}, 40%, 10%)`);\\n- const colorLight = new THREE.Color(`hsl(${hue}, 50%, 18%)`);\\n+ const color = new THREE.Color(`hsl(${hue}, 40%, 14%)`);\\n+ const colorLight = new THREE.Color(`hsl(${hue}, 50%, 25%)`);\\n```\\n\\n### 6. Server must detect 'off' state for Hermes agents without bridge\\n\\nIn `/api/admin/agents`, chat agents with `useHermes === true` that are idle but have **no running bridge instance** should get `status: 'off'`:\\n\\n```javascript\\nif (chat.useHermes === true && status === 'idle' && !chat.isThinking && !chat.isRunning) {\\n    const hasBridge = hermesInstances.some(inst => inst.id === project.id);\\n    if (!hasBridge) status = 'off';\\n}\\n```\\n\\n### 7. Loading overlay never hidden\\n\\nThe `#loading` div is shown initially but has no built-in hide mechanism. You MUST hide it manually after the first successful `rebuildScene()`:\\n\\n```javascript\\nconst loadingEl = document.getElementById('loading');\\nif (loadingEl) loadingEl.style.display = 'none';\\n```\\n\\n### 8. Agent filter hides idle agents\\n\\n`rebuildScene()` originally filtered out `isHermes && status === 'idle'`. The user wants ALL agents visible always. Don't filter — pass `agents` directly as `visibleAgents`.\\n\\n### 9. Status string 'off' breaks CSS classes\\n\\nThe `dot ${s}` class renders `.dot.off`. You MUST add a CSS class for it:\\n\\n```css\\n.agent-row .dot.off { background: #555; box-shadow: none; }\\n.fi-status-indicator .s-dot.off { background: #555; box-shadow: none; }\\n```\\n\\n### 10. Focus panel and label override ghost status\\n\\nThe focus panel and buildLabel have special-case logic for `isGhost` that may break when `status` changes. Always check `isOff` before `isGhost` in the ternary chain if both states should render differently.\\n\\n### 11. Building the statusHue mapping\\n\\nWhen adding new statuses, update ALL of these locations:\\n- `buildMage()` — statusHue, glowColor, satFactor\\n- `buildLabel()` — statusColor, statusText, tag line\\n- `updatePanel()` — tagText, style\\n- `updateFocusPanel()` — label text, status text, dot class, tag\\n- `animate()` — isActive logic\\n- CSS — `.dot.{status}` and `.s-dot.{status}` classes\\n\\n### 12. Template literal escaping in patch\\n\\nThe `html += \\\\`<div class=\\\"...\\\">\\\\`` template literals use escaped double quotes (`\\\\\\\"`). The patch tool has escape-drift issues with these. Use `execute_code` with Python string replacement when editing these lines.\\n\\n### 13. Labels must follow orbiting agents\\n\\nSince the June 2026 orbit update, labels are stored as `mage.userData.label` (a CSS2DObject). The orbit animation block in `animate()` must update both mage position AND label position:\\n\\n```javascript\\nif (ud.label) {\\n  ud.label.position.x = newX;\\n  ud.label.position.z = newZ;\\n}\\n```\\n\\nWithout this, the label stays at the initial position while the agent drifts away.\\n\\n### 14. Server.js needs `import os from 'os'`\\n\\nWhen adding `os.homedir()` calls for the status file path resolution in server.js, add the import at the top:\\n\\n```javascript\\nimport os from 'os';\\n```\\n\\nThe `os` module is a Node.js built-in, no npm dependency needed.\\n\\n### 15. Status files must have a TTL\\n\\nGhost Hermes processes may die without cleaning up their status file. The server enforces a 30s TTL:\\n\\n```javascript\\nconst HERMES_STATUS_TTL = 30000; // 30s\\nif (now - status.timestamp > HERMES_STATUS_TTL) {\\n    fs.unlink(statusPath).catch(() => {});\\n    continue;\\n}\\n```\\n\\n### 16. The status writer must be best-effort\\n\\nThe `_write_status_file()` method in Hermes' `run_agent.py` wraps everything in `try/except Exception: pass`. Never make it throw or block — it runs inside the main conversation loop.\\n\\n## Related Skills\\n\\n- `hermes-bridge-nodejs` — spawning Hermes as subprocess (the bridge side of JP Agents)\\n- Architecture\\n\\n```\\nserver.js (/api/admin/agents)\\n  ├─ chats from sessions (status: off|idle|thinking|running|error)\\n  ├─ hermesBridge instances (status: idle|running)\\n  ├─ external Hermes processes via PowerShell scan\\n  │    └─ enriched by ~/.hermes/status/<pid>.json (live data)\\n  │         ├─ status: thinking|idle\\n  │         ├─ session_title, last_message, tool_name\\n  │         └─ 30s TTL — stale files auto-deleted by server\\n  └─ agents-room.html (Three.js + CSS2DRenderer)\\n       ├─ buildMage() — creates the 3D mage character\\n       ├─ buildLabel() — CSS2D label above each mage\\n       ├─ rebuildScene() — polar layout + orbiting agent placement\\n       ├─ animate() — per-frame state machine + orbit animation\\n       ├─ focusAgent() — click-to-focus camera + info panel\\n       └─ refreshAgents() — poll /api/admin/agents every 8s\\n```\\n\\n## Scene Layout: Single Floor + Polar Project Nodes\\n\\nSince June 2026, the layout changed from per-project stacked floors to a single shared ground floor with project nodes in polar (circular) distribution:\\n\\n```\\n         [Proj C]\\n            |\\n    [Proj B]- -[Proj D]\\n         \\\\   /\\n     [Proj A]   (center: origin)\\n         /   \\\\\\n    [Proj E]- -[Proj F]\\n            |\\n         [Proj G]\\n```\\n\\n### Ground Floor (`createGroundFloor()`)\\n- Single `CircleGeometry(450, 48)` at Y=-2, dark indigo (0x0a0a18)\\n- Outer glow ring with `RingGeometry(448, 460)` at opacity 0.06\\n- Subtle grid helper (900x900, 36 divisions) at opacity 0.05\\n\\n### Project Nodes (`createProjectNode()`)\\nEach project is a small pedestal (not a full floor):\\n- Cylinder base: radius 18/22, height 6, at Y=3\\n- Glowing top platform: radius 16, height 1.5, with emissive\\n- Torus glow ring: radius 20, opacity 0.2, around pedestal top\\n- Light pillar: translucent cylinder (radius 4→12, height 60) at opacity 0.03\\n- Project name label: CSS2DObject at Y=60, centered above pedestal\\n- Orbiting ring indicator: `RingGeometry(36, 38)` at Y=-1, opacity 0.06\\n- Rune sprites (4): floating around pedestal at radius 28\\n\\n### Polar Distribution Formula\\n```javascript\\nconst polarRadius = Math.min(180, 40 + projKeys.length * 35);\\nconst polarAngle = (pIdx / projKeys.length) * Math.PI * 2 - Math.PI / 2;\\nnode.position.set(Math.cos(polarAngle) * polarRadius, 0, Math.sin(polarAngle) * polarRadius);\\n```\\n\\n- First project at -90° (top of circle), evenly spaced\\n- Radius auto-scales: 40 + N*35, capped at 180\\n- Unique hue per project: `(pIdx * 60 + 264) % 360`\\n\\n## Agent Orbiting System\\n\\nSince June 2026, agents orbit around their project node instead of standing in a grid.\\n\\n### Orbit Data (stored in `mage.userData`)\\n```javascript\\nmage.userData.orbitCenter = new THREE.Vector3(px, 0, pz);  // project node center\\nmage.userData.orbitAngle = initialOrbitAngle;                // radians\\nmage.userData.orbitRadius = 42;                              // units\\nmage.userData.orbitSpeed = 0.12 + Math.random() * 0.08;      // rad/s\\nmage.userData.label = labelRef;  // CSS2DObject ref for label following\\n```\\n\\n### Orbit Animation (in `animate()`)\\n```javascript\\nud.orbitAngle += dt * ud.orbitSpeed;\\nconst newX = ud.orbitCenter.x + Math.cos(ud.orbitAngle) * ud.orbitRadius;\\nconst newZ = ud.orbitCenter.z + Math.sin(ud.orbitAngle) * ud.orbitRadius;\\nmage.position.x = newX;\\nmage.position.z = newZ;\\n// Label follows:\\nif (ud.label) { ud.label.position.x = newX; ud.label.position.z = newZ; }\\n```\\n\\n- Orbit speed: 0.12~0.20 rad/s → one full orbit every ~30-50 seconds\\n- Agents bob up/down independently (sinusoidal Y offset)\\n- Labels follow the orbiting agent position (stored ref in userData)\\n\\n## 3-State Visual System (Hermes Agents)\\n\\n| Status | Server trigger | Body | Eyes | Hat tip | Book glow | Aura | Bob |\\n|--------|---------------|------|------|---------|-----------|------|-----|\\n| **off** | `useHermes=true` + no bridge instance | gray/desaturated (satFactor=0.15) | dark gray, no emissive | dark, no emissive | dim, no glow | invisible (opacity=0) | 0.3 units |\\n| **idle** | default / bridge not processing | project hue, normal | soft violet (#7c4dff) glow | subtle glow | soft glow | 0.04±0.02 opacity | 2.0 units |\\n| **thinking** | `chat.isThinking=true` or status file says \\\"thinking\\\" | project hue, normal | cyan (#00e5ff) glow | bright glow | full glow | active effects | 2.0 units |\\n| **running** | `chat.isRunning=true` | project hue, normal | purple (#a855f7) glow | bright glow | full glow | full active (orbs, beam, rune circle, particles) | 2.0 units |\\n\\n### Animation State Machine (`animate()`)\\n\\n```javascript\\nconst isGhost = ud.isGhost;\\nconst isOff = ud.isOff;\\nconst isActive = !isGhost && !isOff && (ud.agentStatus === 'running' || ud.agentStatus === 'thinking');\\n\\nif (isGhost) { /* ethereal only: ghostAura + tip flicker */ return; }\\n// Non-ghost: aura / glowLight / outerAura / innerGlow / coreGlow /\\n//   groundRing / energyRings / beam / runeCircle / orbitOrbs / particles\\n// Each has: isActive branch (full), isOff branch (zero), else (idle subtle)\\n```\\n\\n## Ghost Agents (External Hermes Processes)\\n\\n### Detection Pipeline (server.js)\\n\\n```\\nPowerShell scan ──→ PID list ──→ match against ──→ Enrich with live data\\n(WMI Win32_Process)              ~/.hermes/status/*.json     from status files\\n```\\n\\n### PowerShell detection\\n```javascript\\nconst { stdout } = await execPromise(\\n  'powershell -NoProfile -Command \\\"Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"name=\\\\\\\\'hermes.exe\\\\\\\\'\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" | Select-Object ProcessId,CommandLine,WorkingDirectory | ConvertTo-Json\\\"',\\n  { timeout: 5000 }\\n);\\n```\\n\\n### Hermes Status File Protocol (`~/.hermes/status/<pid>.json`)\\nEach running Hermes process writes its own PID-named file with:\\n```json\\n{\\n  \\\"pid\\\": 1234,\\n  \\\"status\\\": \\\"thinking\\\",\\n  \\\"model\\\": \\\"deepseek-v4-flash\\\",\\n  \\\"session_id\\\": \\\"20260529_...\\\",\\n  \\\"session_title\\\": \\\"Project Name\\\",\\n  \\\"tool_name\\\": \\\"patch\\\",\\n  \\\"last_message\\\": \\\"Editando el archivo...\\\",\\n  \\\"timestamp\\\": 1780031818115,\\n  \\\"started_at\\\": 1780031818000\\n}\\n```\\n\\n- Written at the start of each conversation loop iteration → status=\\\"thinking\\\"\\n- Written at the end of each conversation → status=\\\"idle\\\"\\n- Stale files (>30s old) auto-deleted by `server.js`\\n- Written best-effort (silent on failure, never blocks)\\n- Each process writes its own file (PID-unique, no collision)\\n- See `references/hermes-status-file-writer.md` for full protocol\\n\\n### Ghost Mage Rendering\\n- Semi-transparent (opacity 0.45)\\n- Cyan-colored (#00e5ff) with ethereal aura\\n- No book/pedestal\\n- Ethereal aura sphere (opacity 0.20, pulsing + scaling)\\n- Tagged `👻 GHOST` in panels\\n\\n### Ghost Label Richer Info (since June 2026)\\nNow shows real data from status files:\\n- **Name**: uses `sessionTitle` if available (e.g. \\\"👻 Polar Node Config\\\")\\n- **Status**: real status (\\\"⟳ pensando\\\", \\\"◉ idle\\\") instead of always \\\"👻 externo\\\"\\n- **Tool name**: shows \\\"⚡ patch\\\" when agent is running a tool\\n- **Last message preview**: first 80 chars of the last message\\n- **Focus panel**: sessionId tag, toolName tag, real content in last message field\\n\\n## Common Pitfalls\\n\\n### 1. `animate()` MUST be called to start the render loop\\n\\nThe `animate()` function starts with `requestAnimationFrame(animate)` to chain frames, but **nobody invokes it the first time**. You must call `animate();` explicitly after setup:\\n\\n```javascript\\n// ─── START ───\\nrefreshAgents();\\nsetInterval(refreshAgents, 8000);\\nanimate();  // ← THIS IS MANDATORY\\n```\\n\\nWithout this, the canvas exists, the scene is built, but `renderer.render()` is never called — the canvas stays pure black. This is the #1 cause of \\\"black screen, no errors\\\" in this codebase.\\n\\n### 2. `toneMapping` crushes dark colors to black\\n\\n`renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping` (the original value) applies a filmic curve that maps dark colors to zero on some GPU/driver combos. With scene colors at 10-25% lightness, ghosts at 0.25 opacity, and aura at 0.08 opacity, everything becomes invisible:\\n\\n```javascript\\n// ❌ BAD — everything disappears on some GPUs\\nrenderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;\\nrenderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.2;\\n\\n// ✅ SAFE — linear output preserves dark colors\\nrenderer.toneMapping = THREE.NoToneMapping;\\n```\\n\\n### 3. Scene background must be visible, not pure black\\n\\n`scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x06060e)` is indistinguishable from black on most monitors. Use a dark-but-visible color:\\n\\n```diff\\n- scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x06060e);\\n+ scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x0e0e28);  // dark indigo, visibly different from black\\n```\\n\\n### 4. Ghost opacity too low to render\\n\\nGhost mages start at `bodyOpacity = 0.25`. With ACES tone mapping and camera distance, they become invisible. Increase to 0.45:\\n\\n```diff\\n- const bodyOpacity = isGhost ? 0.25 : 1;\\n+ const bodyOpacity = isGhost ? 0.45 : 1;\\n```\\n\\nAlso increase the ghost aura sphere:\\n```diff\\n- opacity: 0.08,\\n+ opacity: 0.20,\\n```\\n\\n### 5. Floor colors too dark\\n\\nThe floor platform and ring border use very low lightness values (10-18%). Combined with ACES tone mapping, they don't render. Bump them:\\n\\n```diff\\n- const color = new THREE.Color(`hsl(${hue}, 40%, 10%)`);\\n- const colorLight = new THREE.Color(`hsl(${hue}, 50%, 18%)`);\\n+ const color = new THREE.Color(`hsl(${hue}, 40%, 14%)`);\\n+ const colorLight = new THREE.Color(`hsl(${hue}, 50%, 25%)`);\\n```\\n\\n### 6. Server must detect 'off' state for Hermes agents without bridge\\n\\nIn `/api/admin/agents`, chat agents with `useHermes === true` that are idle but have **no running bridge instance** should get `status: 'off'`:\\n\\n```javascript\\nif (chat.useHermes === true && status === 'idle' && !chat.isThinking && !chat.isRunning) {\\n    const hasBridge = hermesInstances.some(inst => inst.id === project.id);\\n    if (!hasBridge) status = 'off';\\n}\\n```\\n\\n### 7. Loading overlay never hidden\\n\\nThe `#loading` div is shown initially but has no built-in hide mechanism. You MUST hide it manually after the first successful `rebuildScene()`:\\n\\n```javascript\\nconst loadingEl = document.getElementById('loading');\\nif (loadingEl) loadingEl.style.display = 'none';\\n```\\n\\n### 8. Agent filter hides idle agents\\n\\n`rebuildScene()` originally filtered out `isHermes && status === 'idle'`. The user wants ALL agents visible always. Don't filter — pass `agents` directly as `visibleAgents`.\\n\\n### 9. Status string 'off' breaks CSS classes\\n\\nThe `dot ${s}` class renders `.dot.off`. You MUST add a CSS class for it:\\n\\n```css\\n.agent-row .dot.off { background: #555; box-shadow: none; }\\n.fi-status-indicator .s-dot.off { background: #555; box-shadow: none; }\\n```\\n\\n### 10. Focus panel and label override ghost status\\n\\nThe focus panel and buildLabel have special-case logic for `isGhost` that may break when `status` changes. Always check `isOff` before `isGhost` in the ternary chain if both states should render differently.\\n\\n### 11. Building the statusHue mapping\\n\\nWhen adding new statuses, update ALL of these locations:\\n- `buildMage()` — statusHue, glowColor, satFactor\\n- `buildLabel()` — statusColor, statusText, tag line\\n- `updatePanel()` — tagText, style\\n- `updateFocusPanel()` — label text, status text, dot class, tag\\n- `animate()` — isActive logic\\n- CSS — `.dot.{status}` and `.s-dot.{status}` classes\\n\\n### 12. Template literal escaping in patch\\n\\nThe `html += \\\\`<div class=\\\"...\\\">\\\\`` template literals use escaped double quotes (`\\\\\\\"`). The patch tool has escape-drift issues with these. Use `execute_code` with Python string replacement when editing these lines.\\n\\n### 13. Labels must follow orbiting agents\\n\\nSince the June 2026 orbit update, labels are stored as `mage.userData.label` (a CSS2DObject). The orbit animation block in `animate()` must update both mage position AND label position:\\n\\n```javascript\\nif (ud.label) {\\n  ud.label.position.x = newX;\\n  ud.label.position.z = newZ;\\n}\\n```\\n\\nWithout this, the label stays at the initial position while the agent drifts away.\\n\\n### 14. Server.js needs `import os from 'os'`\\n\\nWhen adding `os.homedir()` calls for the status file path resolution in server.js, add the import at the top:\\n\\n```javascript\\nimport os from 'os';\\n```\\n\\nThe `os` module is a Node.js built-in, no npm dependency needed.\\n\\n### 15. Status files must have a TTL\\n\\nGhost Hermes processes may die without cleaning up their status file. The server enforces a 30s TTL:\\n\\n```javascript\\nconst HERMES_STATUS_TTL = 30000; // 30s\\nif (now - status.timestamp > HERMES_STATUS_TTL) {\\n    fs.unlink(statusPath).catch(() => {});\\n    continue;\\n}\\n```\\n\\n### 16. The status writer must be best-effort\\n\\nThe `_write_status_file()` method in Hermes' `run_agent.py` wraps everything in `try/except Exception: pass`. Never make it throw or block — it runs inside the main conversation loop.\\n\\n## Related Skills\\n\\n- `hermes-bridge-nodejs` — spawning Hermes as subprocess (the bridge side of JP Agents)\\n-
